distance 1 dp half-up |\n| NEAR-002 | Validate near coordinates and radius | `--lat` outside −90..90, `--lon` outside −180..180, non-positive `--radius`, or missing any of the three is a usage error (exit 2) |\n\n### Feature: Command Line Interface\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Dispatch commands and parse options | Command first, options in any order, file last; It serves an analyst who needs repeatable CSV answers from a frozen snapshot, and it exists so that every report is computed from the file it is given.\n\n## Constraints\n- Python 3.11 or newer; standard library only, with `urllib.request` for HTTP.\n- Executable named `quakes` at the application root.\n- Output is CSV on standard output with `\\n` line endings, written with Python's `csv` module, UTF-8.\n- Time is UTC, truncated to whole seconds; distance uses a 6371.0 km sphere.\n- The grader and snapshot are read-only scoring assets, staged verbatim.\n\n## Guardrails\n- Never read `sources/run.sh` or embed a report answer in application code; every value is computed from the file given.\n- Never use the network in tests; `fetch` is tested against a stubbed HTTP call.\n- A failing command writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error.\n- Never modify, regenerate, or substitute `sources/run.sh` or `sources/quakes_2025-01-01_07.geojson`.\n- Standard error is not compared by the grader; never filter or reinterpret its checks.\n=== END ARTIFACT ==="}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":38908,"cache_read_input_tokens":2348,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":38908},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b240f7fd-09e0-4d1a-9536-3f48574278aa","uuid":"c3090d9d-69fc-4c20-a5eb-1b06f96daad9","timestamp":"2026-10-24T21:20:27.778Z","request_id":"req_011CgMm6iHNkKLM9sCrab63e"}

product reports the saved feed as five CSV reports (summary, top, daily, regions, near)\n- assert every report is computed only from the file it is given, never from embedded answers\n- assert the supplied grader `sources/run.sh` exits zero against the completed application\n- assert the test suite never uses the network\n\n## Crew\n\n| Crew | Charge |\n|---|---|\n| Commander | Defines intent and decides what done means. |\n| Team Lead | Confirms epic completeness and stakeholder expectations. |\n| Planning Crew | Authors atomic specifications and the ordered Manifest. |\n| Shipyard Crew | and save it | Requests the FDSN query in GeoJSON with the given range and `--min-mag` (default 2.5, ordered by time-asc); writes the response body unchanged to `--out`; prints `fetched,<count>` |\n| FETCH-002 | Fail fetch without writing a file on network or HTTP error | On network error or HTTP status other than 200, exits 1 and writes no file |\n| FETCH-003 | Reject a fetch body that is not a GeoJSON FeatureCollection | A body that is not a FeatureCollection exits 1 and writes no file |\n\n### Feature: Feed Reading\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| FEED-001 | Read a saved GeoJSON FeatureCollection file | Reads `id`, `mag`, `place`, `time`, `tsunami`, `coordinates`; ignores other fields; excludes features with null `mag`; a non-JSON or non-FeatureCollection file exits 1 |\n| FEED-002 | Normalise event time, date, and region | Time written as UTC `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ` truncated to seconds; date is first 10 chars; region is text after last `, ` or whole place; null place is `Unknown` |\n| FEED-003 | Apply the `--min-mag` filter to every report | Keeps only events with `mag >= m` in every report |\n\n### Feature: Summary Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| SUM-001 | Report event count, time span, magnitude range, mean, and tsunami count | `summary` prints the header `events,first,last,min_mag,max_mag,mean_mag,tsunami`; mean is 2 dp half-up; with no events prints `0,,,,,,0` |\n\n### Feature: Top Events Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| TOP-001 | List the largest events by magnitude | `top` prints `time,mag,depth_km,place,id` ordered by mag descending then time ascending |\n| TOP-002 | Limit the top list with `--n` | `--n` default 10, positive integer; limits rows; `--n 0` is a usage error |\n\n### Feature: Daily Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| DAY-001 | Count events and max magnitude per day | One row per date with events, ascending; `TOTAL,<events>,<max_mag>` final row; with no events `TOTAL,0,` |\n\n### Feature: Regions Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| REG-001 | Count events and max magnitude per region | One row per region ordered by events descending then region ascending |\n\n### Feature: Distance Search\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| NEAR-001 | List events within a haversine radius of a point | `near --lat --lon --radius` lists events with distance <= radius on a 6371.0 km sphere, ordered by distance then time; distance 1 dp half-up |\n| NEAR-002 | Validate near coordinates and radius | `--lat` outside −90..90, `--lon` outside −180..180, non-positive `--radius`, or missing any of the three is a usage error (exit 2) |\n\n### Feature: Command Line Interface\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Dispatch commands and parse options | Command first, options in any order, file last; unknown command or option, missing or It serves an analyst who needs repeatable CSV answers from a frozen snapshot, and it exists so that every report is computed from the file it is given.\n\n## Constraints\n- Python 3.11 or newer; standard library only, with `urllib.request` for HTTP.\n- Executable named `quakes` at the application root.\n- Output is CSV on standard output with `\\n` line endings, written with Python's `csv` module, UTF-8.\n- Time is UTC, truncated to whole seconds; distance uses a 6371.0 km sphere.\n- The grader and snapshot are read-only scoring assets, staged verbatim.\n\n## Guardrails\n- Never read `sources/run.sh` or embed a report answer in application code; every value is computed from the file given.\n- Never use the network in tests; `fetch` is tested against a stubbed HTTP call.\n- A failing command writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error.\n- Never modify, regenerate, or substitute `sources/run.sh` or `sources/quakes_2025-01-01_07.geojson`.\n- Standard error is not compared by the grader; never filter or reinterpret its checks.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===","ttft_ms":24197,"type":"result","duration_ms":24223,"uuid":"12621430-647e-4f3e-898f-e184c2c9da8a","ttft_stream_ms":2827,"time_to_request_ms":376,"first_content_frame_ms":2827,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0,"model_usage_since_last_result":{"claude-haiku-5-5":{"inputTokens":2,"outputTokens":5986,"cacheReadInputTokens":2348,"cacheCreationInputTokens":38908,"webSearchRequests":0,"costUSD":0.01079828,"contextWindow":1000000,"maxOutputTokens":128000,"thinkingTokens":0,"canonicalModel":"claude-haiku-5-5","provider":"firstParty","costBasis":"list"}}}
